Covers the CData JDBC Driver edition: reusing the same jar cdata-cli already downloaded and activated, putting it on the classpath (or adding it as a Maven/Gradle dependency), and connecting via JDBC (DriverManager, the jdbc:&lt;source&gt;: URL, PreparedStatement). Use when the user asks to "debug the crash", "run under cdb", "get a stack trace", "why does it crash on startup", "analyze the .dmp", or when reading source alone did not reveal the cause. - **cddc-osint**: Open-source intelligence for CTF -- find people, places, and data from PUBLIC sources. Do not use when the data is inside a provided artifact -- use cddc-forensics; OSINT often chains with stego (cddc-forensics) or unicode encoding (cddc-misc). - **cddc-ot-ics**: Solve CDDC Operational-Technology / ICS challenges by identifying the industrial protocol, mapping the process, and driving it to a goal state. Do not use when it is a generic non-industrial network capture -- use cddc-network or cddc-forensics instead. ([arvinty/cddc-image](https://github.com/arvinty/cddc-image/tree/HEAD/skills/cddc-skills/cddc-ot-ics))
- **cddc-pwn**: Binary exploitation techniques for CDDC 2026 — stack overflow, ROP/ret2libc, format string, glibc & custom-allocator heap, FSOP, and Linux kernel pwn. Use when you have a native ELF (or PE) target or remote service that crashes on input, leaks/corrupts memory, or exposes a buffer overflow, ROP, heap bug, use-after-free, double-free, format string, canary, custom allocator, race condition, or kernel module. Do not use when the blocker is understanding what the binary does — use cddc-reverse for that — and not for pure web, crypto, or forensics challenges. ([arvinty/cddc-image](https://github.com/arvinty/cddc-image/tree/HEAD/skills/cddc-skills/cddc-pwn))
- **cddc-reverse**: Reverse engineering for CDDC 2026 — recover what a binary/program does and how it checks a flag. Do not use when you already understand the bug and need to exploit a memory-corruption/service -- use cddc-pwn instead. ([arvinty/cddc-image](https://github.com/arvinty/cddc-image/tree/HEAD/skills/cddc-skills/cddc-reverse))
